# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-10T11:49:40+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Mon, 09 Nov 2026 21:58:00 -0300 — Judiciário e MPU querem R$ 533 milhões para pagar penduricalhos
  O Poder Judiciário e o Ministério Público da União (MPU) informaram ao Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) que precisam de R$ 533 milhões extras para realizar o pagamento das verbas indenizatórias, conhecidas como penduricalhos, que foram validadas pela Corte. A quantia consta em um pedido de esclarecimento apresentado pela Advocacia-Geral da União (AGU) após o Judiciário e o MPU constatarem que não há dinheiro previsto no Orçamento de 2026 para pagar os benefícios. O Judiciário precisa de R$ 208,9 milhões para os pagamentos. No MP, a despesa calculada é de R$ 324 milhões. Notícias relacionadas: STF ordena suspensão e devolução de penduricalhos "exorbitantes" . Confira penduricalhos cortados e mantidos após decisão do Supremo. A falta de recursos pode comprometer, por exemplo, o pagamento da Pa
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/judiciario-e-mpu-querem-r-533-milhoes-para-pagar-penduricalhos
- Mon, 09 Nov 2026 20:31:00 -0300 — Chuvas deixam feridos, desabrigados e casas destelhadas no Sul
  Dezenas de cidades do Sul do país organizaram força-tarefa para atendimento à população afetadas pelas chuvas que atingiram a região entre quinta-feira (5) e sábado (7). Há distribuição de cestas básicas, colchões, kits de higiene e telhas. Os três estados registraram chuvas fortes, vendavais, enxurradas e precipitação com granizo, influenciados pelo El Niño. Rio Grande do Sul Notícias relacionadas: Chuvas permanecem intensas no Sul e aumentam no Sudeste e Centro-Oeste. Em Bento Gonçalves (RS), três pessoas ficaram feridas e cerca de 1.500 casas foram destelhadas. Cacique Doble e Pontão, também no Rio Grande do Sul, tiveram respectivamente 75 e oito casas alagadas. Segundo a Defesa Civil gaúcha, há 42 pessoas desalojadas e quatro desabrigadas no estado. Santa Catarina No estado de Santa Ca

Back A Robotic Arm That Can Walk Across Spacecraft? Rocket Lab Built It Share this Future space missions could soon get a big leg up — or arm, rather — thanks to robotics developed by Rocket Lab in collaboration with NASA. Unlike traditional robotic arms that remain fixed in place, this version is designed to move autonomously across spacecraft and operate in zero or partial gravity, performing critical tasks like repairs, upgrades, and assembly. Rocket Lab Robotics (formerly Motiv Space Systems) developed the arm with versatility in mind. It can manipulate objects, use tools, and even perform complex tasks like repairing or upgrading space systems. Funded through NASA’s Small Business Innovation Re
  https://www.rocketlabusa.com/updates/a-robotic-arm-that-can-walk-across-spacecraft-rocket-lab-built-it/

The ‘We're Not Hawking Around’ mission launched from Rocket Lab Launch Complex 1 in New Zealand at 8:11 p.m. NZST on 21 October 2026, to deliver three HAWK satellites to a 520km low Earth orbit. The Copernicus Hyperspectral Imaging Mission for the Environment (CHIME) is advancing its development to be ready for launch. CHIME will provide routine hyperspectral observations from space across the visible to shortwave infrared spectral range (400-2500 nm), supporting enhanced environmental services in areas such as food security, sustainable agriculture, biodiversity monitoring, soil characterisation, mineral resources and water quality. With a swath of approximately 130 km and a spatial sampling distance of 30 m, CHIME will complement Copernicus Sentinel-2 and extend Europe's Earth observation capabilities through operational imaging spectroscopy. - Fri, 06 Nov 2026 14:42:30 +0000 — Earth's Gravity May Have Kept the Early Moon's Volcanic Heart Beating
  We typically think of the Moon as a static, floating object that has remained the same for basically its entire lifetime, with an occasional asteroid impact serving as an exception. That understanding is grounded in physics - the Moon should have cooled rapidly after forming when a huge protoplanet hit the early Earth around 4.5 billion years ago. But, some lunar material returned from the Chang’e-5 mission was notably formed only around two billion years ago, yet appeared to be volcanic in origin. So how did a world that was supposed to have settled down without volcanism remain hot enough to create these materials more than two billion years after its violent birth?   https://www.universetoday.com/articles/earths-gravity-may-have-kept-the-early-moons-volcanic-heart-beating

- Mon, 09 Nov 2026 21:58:00 -0300 — Judiciário e MPU querem R$ 533 milhões para pagar penduricalhos
  O Poder Judiciário e o Ministério Público da União (MPU) informaram ao Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) que precisam de R$ 533 milhões extras para realizar o pagamento das verbas indenizatórias, conhecidas como penduricalhos, que foram validadas pela Corte. A quantia consta em um pedido de esclarecimento apresentado pela Advocacia-Geral da União (AGU) após o Judiciário e o MPU constatarem que não há dinheiro previsto no Orçamento de 2026 para pagar os benefícios. O Judiciário precisa de R$ 208,9 milhões para os pagamentos. No MP, a despesa calculada é de R$ 324 milhões. Notícias relacionadas: STF ordena suspensão e devolução de penduricalhos "exorbitantes" . - Mon, 09 Nov 2026 20:31:00 -0300 — Chuvas deixam feridos, desabrigados e casas destelhadas no Sul
  Dezenas de cidades do Sul do país organizaram força-tarefa para atendimento à população afetadas pelas chuvas que atingiram a região entre quinta-feira (5) e sábado (7). Há distribuição de cestas básicas, colchões, kits de higiene e telhas. Os três estados registraram chuvas fortes, vendavais, enxurradas e precipitação com granizo, influenciados pelo El Niño. Rio Grande do Sul Notícias relacionadas: Chuvas permanecem intensas no Sul e aumentam no Sudeste e Centro-Oeste. Em Bento Gonçalves (RS), três pessoas ficaram feridas e cerca de 1.500 casas foram destelhadas. Cacique Doble e Pontão, também no Rio Grande do Sul, tiveram respectivamente 75 e oito casas alagadas. Segundo a Defesa Civil gaúcha, há 42 pessoas desalojadas e quatro desabrigadas no estado. Santa Catarina No estado de Santa Ca

  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/

  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions. - undated — Apollo and Artemis Astronauts Share the Stage at Gala
  Apollo and Artemis Astronauts Share the Stage at Gala Skip to content CAPE CANAVERAL, FL—Twenty-eight astronauts—including two Apollo veterans—and one very large Saturn V shared a room at Kennedy Space Center on Friday night for the Artemis Celebration Gala, held the evening before NASA’s MAX POWER event. NASA Administrator Jared Isaacman described the astronauts as “a living connection between those who first taught humanity how to reach another world and those who will learn how to stay there.” Apollo 16 moonwalker Charlie Duke and Apollo 9 lunar module pilot Rusty Schweickart were the evening’s guests of honor. Organizers also honored Apollo-era engineers in attendance, including JoAnn Morgan, Poppy Northcutt, and Jay Honeycutt.&nbsp; Apollo 9.0: Schweickart was joined onstage by the Artemis III crew, including backup Bob Hines, who stood in for an absent Andre Douglas.   https://payloadspace.com/apollo-and-artemis-astronauts-share-the-stage-at-gala/
- undated — Canada’s Medium-Lift Rocket Ambitions Confirmed In Budget
  Canada’s Medium-Lift Rocket Ambitions Confirmed In Budget Skip to content Canada is ready to give its young launch industry a boost. The country will upgrade its rockets to medium lift as part of its Launch the North initiative, which is aiming to support sovereign rockets flying from at least two nascent spaceports on Canada’s east coast. The timeline for the upgrade is not clear, however; the smaller rockets are expected to get off the ground in 2028. Canada’s C$142B ($100B) federal budget five-year-spending projection, released late Thursday, officially put money behind the previously announced rocket spending plans. The budget also promised new rockets, a sovereign “rocket-propulsion ecosystem,” and defense-department backed launch and testing. Billions of dollars were awarded or soon will be for cargo landers, a nuclear-powered lunar rover, launch and transportation, as well as architecture and infrastructure capabilities. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. - undated — CSA awards ABB $1.5 million to develop a Mars methane detector
  Mars methane detector: CSA awards ABB $1.5 million Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content NASA/JPL-Caltech/MSSS The Canadian Space Agency (CSA) said it has awarded Quebec City-based ABB Inc. a contract worth about $1.5 million to further develop an instrument that could detect trace amounts of methane directly in the Martian atmosphere. The instrument is called the Martian Atmospheric Gas Evolution Experiment – Inter-Cavity Optical Spectroscopy, or MAGE-ICOS. Optical spectroscopy is a technique that identifies gases by the way they absorb light. The contract was awarded through the CSA&#8217;s Space Technology Development Program (STDP). The CSA says methane on Mars could offer clues about the planet&#8217;s past and present, including whether it may once have supported life. Signals suggesting the gas is there have been detected.   LUNR Aerospace goes public before its first engine test Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content An artist’s rendering of LUNR-01, LUNR Aerospace’s planned small launch vehicle. Credit: LUNR Aerospace LUNR Aerospace, a Toronto startup that wants to launch small satellites to orbit by 2029, has come out of stealth in an unusual way. Instead of announcing an engine test or a funding round, it went public and listed its shares on the Canadian Securities Exchange (CSE) on Oct. 27, under the symbol FLY . LUNR filed its prospectus, a detailed disclosure document a company needs before its shares can trade publicly, but didn&#8217;t sell any new shares , so the listing didn&#8217;t raise any new funds. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . - undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### Austria in Space (national space platform) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php
- undated — Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS : Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag.
  Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS - Austria in Space Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag. Pegasus ist jetzt bereits vier Jahre aktiv. (Perihelion ) PEGASUS ist Teil des Satellitenprojekts QB50 und wurde 2017 von Indien aus in die Umlaufbahn gebracht. Eines der Hauptziele des Projekts QB50 war es, Studierenden auf der ganzen Welt die Möglichkeit zu geben, einen Satelliten zu entwickeln und zu betreiben. Das Ergebnis dieses Projekts in Österreich war PEGASUS, der von Studierenden der Fachhochschule Wiener Neustadt, der Technischen Universität Wien und der Universität Wien in Zusammenarbeit mit dem Österreichischen Weltraumforum entwickelt wurde. Der Satellit ist noch immer im Einsatz und überträgt weiterhin Daten. Projekt: QB50, ein Netzwerk von 36 Nanosatellit
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/happybirthdaypegasus.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm
  https://news.belgium.be/fr/node/42322/pdf

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu National Mourning It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, a
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space
- undated — The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION]
  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu National Mourning Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Przełomowy lot Starshipa. Znamy datę i szczegóły
  Przełomowy lot Starshipa. Znamy datę i szczegóły Menu Żałoba narodowa Kilka dni temu SpaceX poinformowało, że piętnasty lot testowy systemu Starship/Super Heavy (Flight 15) odbędzie się nie wcześniej niż w piątek, 13 listopada. 75-minutowe okno startowe otworzy się o godz. 23:45 czasu polskiego . Przeprowadzenie misji w tym terminie będzie jednak uzależnione od uzyskania wymaganych zgód regulacyjnych. RPA razpis je namenjen samo podjetjem in akademskim ter raziskovalnim organizacijam s sedežem v Sloveniji. Namen razpisa je krepitev slovenskega vesoljskega sektorja in spodbujanje vključitve obstoječih ter novih podjetij in organizacij, ki še niso aktivna na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Senators Introduce Legislation to Streamline Satellite Licensing - Via Satellite : Home » Government/Military » Senators Introduce Legislation to Streamline Satellite Licensing --> U.S. Capitol Building. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Dr. Farhan: The Kuwait National Space Research Center is a national platform for excellence in space research. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. #### Kenya Law (government, en, text-reader)

- undated — The Kenya Space Agency Order
  ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. Establishment of the Agency (1)There is hereby established a state corporation to be known as the Kenya Space Agency.(2)The Agency shall be a body corporate with perpetual succession and a common seal and shall, in its corporate name, be capable of—(a)suing and being sued;(b)taking, purchasing or otherwise acquiring, holding, charging or disposing of movable and immovable property;(c)receiving, investing, borrowing and lending money; and(d)doing or performing any such other thing or act, including entering into such contracts as may be
  https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. #### Royal Centre for Remote Sensing (CRTS) (space_office, fr, text-reader)

- undated — Centre Royal de Télédétection Spatiale
  ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma

- undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  About ZINGSA &#8212; Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency Phone +263 8677009885 Email publicrelations@zingsa.ac.zw Hours Mon&#8211;Fri, 8am&#8211;4pm Contact Us About The ZINGSA story Promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial technologies for Zimbabwe&#8217;s development. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De

During steady-state operation, the engine performance is normal and consistent with nominal predictions. The test duration was 200 seconds and the performance of the engine was satisfactory and consistent with the pre-test predictions. Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday.   Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ In Focus Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ MONDAY, SEPTEMBER 05, 2022 | THE NATION สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. - undated — Anthropic IPO 前夕推 Fable 5.2 與 Mythos 5.2，效能提升且降價 20%
  Anthropic IPO 前夕推 Fable 5.2 與 Mythos 5.2，效能提升且降價 20% | TechNews 科技新報 預期即將掛牌上市的前夕，Anthropic 9 日推出 Claude Fable 5.2 與 Claude Mythos 5.2 兩款全新先進模型，在程式設計、推理、視覺等方面有所提升。 Fable 5.2 成為 Anthropic 對外公開的最強大模型，同時也是 Anthropic 至今在「開放性技術工作」效能表現最好的模型。在多項針對代理式 AI 能力的基準測試中，Fable 5.2 均超越 Anthropic 先前的模型。Fable 5.2 與 Mythos 5.2 採用相同的底層模型，但 Fable 5.2 在網路安全與生物研究能力設有更為嚴格的限制，並對外開放使用。 Fable 5.2 效能提升的同時，運行成本卻較先前的模型更低。Anthropic 指出，Fable 5.2 生成內容的速度最高可較 Fable 5.1 與 Mythos 5.1 快上 30%。同時，這兩款模型的價格也較前一代低了 20%，API 定價為每百萬個輸入 token 收費 8 美元、每百萬個輸出 token 收費 40 美元，略低於 OpenAI 的 GPT-6 Astra。 ▲ Fable 5.2 的基準測試表現。 儘管基準測試與定價是各款模型相互比較時的衡量指標，但每當一款新模型問世，各家 AI 公司更會大力宣揚新模型究竟能做些什麼。 Anthropic 聲稱，曾交付 Fable 5.2 一項任務：從零開始建構一套作業系統原型。Fable 5.2 隨即協調超過 1,000 個子代理，約 8 天內寫出一套可實際運作的作業系統及其程式碼，能在筆電順利開機，並具備登入畫面、檔案儲存機制，以及對網路連線與其他基本功能的支援。 Anthropic 甚至運用 Fable 5.2 破解兩道關於黑洞物理學的問題：首先是發現黑洞在小型宇宙的形成如同大型宇宙一樣突然，接著據稱找出物理學家在一個簡化宇宙中排除各項量子重力理論的技術極限。Anthropic 已發表研究論
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/10/anthropic-introduces-claude-fable-5-2-and-claude-mythos-5-2/
